# Legacy operator-provided file index

> **Reference only.** These files came from the old project. This index does not approve them for CollisionSpike v2. Review any concept with a human operator before use.

This index covers the 1585 original files found under `docs/reference/operator-provided/` on 2026-07-23, including the hidden Office lock file. Files in this `guide/` folder are excluded from the count.

The category is based on the file's original purpose, not its extension. Detailed comparisons with current v2 are in the category guides linked from [README.md](./README.md).
